```python
import datetime as dt

from jamstockex import refresh, store
from jamstockex.config import JAMAICA, default_config
from jamstockex.fetch import BlockedError, FetchError
from tests.fakes import FakeFetcher, pages
from tests.helpers import JSE_URL, fixture_html

SETTINGS = default_config()
NOV_9 = dt.datetime(2026, 11, 9, 18, 0, tzinfo=JAMAICA)    # Monday, after the close
NOV_10 = dt.datetime(2026, 11, 10, 18, 0, tzinfo=JAMAICA)
NOV_11 = dt.datetime(2026, 11, 11, 18, 0, tzinfo=JAMAICA)
NOV_12 = dt.datetime(2026, 11, 12, 18, 0, tzinfo=JAMAICA)
NOV_13 = dt.datetime(2026, 11, 13, 18, 0, tzinfo=JAMAICA)
NOV_16 = dt.datetime(2026, 11, 16, 18, 0, tzinfo=JAMAICA)
DEC_24 = dt.datetime(2026, 12, 24, 18, 0, tzinfo=JAMAICA)


def run(state, now, overrides=None, **kwargs):
    fetcher = FakeFetcher(pages(overrides))
    report = refresh.run_refresh(state, fetcher, now, SETTINGS, **kwargs)
    return report, fetcher


def test_first_run_fetches_everything():
    state = store.State()
    report, fetcher = run(state, NOV_9)
    assert fetcher.paths == [
        "/trading/trade-quotes/?market=50&date=2026-11-09",
        "/listings/listed-companies/?market=31",
        "/listings/listed-companies/?market=22",
        "/listings/listed-companies/?market=51",
        "/listings/listed-companies/?market=49",
        "/trading/corporate-actions/?instrumentCode=all-stocks&fromDate=2026-01-01&thruDate=2027-11-09",
    ]
    assert report.exit_code == 0 and report.errors == {} and report.requests == 6
    assert report.listings_reason == "no stored listings"
    assert report.datasets == {"listings": "updated", "quotes": "updated", "indices": "updated",
                               "corporate_actions": "updated"}
    assert state.companies["9001"].quote.close == 12.5
    assert len(state.indices) == 2
    assert {a.instrument_code for a in state.actions} == {"9001", "9002"}
    assert state.status["quotes"].as_of == dt.date(2026, 11, 9)


def test_before_the_close_requests_the_previous_trading_day():
    state = store.State()
    _, fetcher = run(state, dt.datetime(2026, 11, 9, 9, 0, tzinfo=JAMAICA))  # Monday morning
    assert fetcher.paths[0] == "/trading/trade-quotes/?market=50&date=2026-11-06"


def test_same_close_again_makes_no_requests():
    state = store.State()
    run(state, NOV_9)
    report, fetcher = run(state, NOV_9 + dt.timedelta(hours=3))
    assert fetcher.paths == [] and not report.attempted
    assert report.text().endswith("Nothing is due: the stored data is up to date.")


def test_next_day_needs_only_two_requests():
    state = store.State()
    run(state, NOV_9)
    report, fetcher = run(state, NOV_10)
    assert fetcher.paths == [
        "/trading/trade-quotes/?market=50&date=2026-11-10",
        "/trading/corporate-actions/?instrumentCode=all-stocks&fromDate=2026-10-10&thruDate=2027-11-10",
    ]
    assert report.requests == 2 and report.listings_reason is None
    assert report.datasets["listings"] == "skipped"


def test_listings_refresh_when_due_by_age():
    state = store.State()
    run(state, NOV_9)
    report, fetcher = run(state, NOV_16)
    assert sum(p.startswith("/listings/") for p in fetcher.paths) == 4
    assert report.listings_reason.startswith("last refresh")


def test_unknown_instrument_in_quotes_triggers_listings_refresh_once():
    state = store.State()
    run(state, NOV_9)
    gamma = fixture_html("trade-quotes.html").replace("instrument=9002", "instrument=9003")
    report, fetcher = run(state, NOV_10, {"/trading/trade-quotes/": gamma})
    assert sum(p.startswith("/listings/") for p in fetcher.paths) == 4
    assert "unknown instrument" in report.listings_reason
    assert any("9003" in w for w in report.warnings)
    assert state.unmatched == {"9003"}
    report, fetcher = run(state, NOV_11, {"/trading/trade-quotes/": gamma})
    assert report.requests == 2 and report.listings_reason is None


def test_force_listings():
    state = store.State()
    run(state, NOV_9)
    report, fetcher = run(state, NOV_10, force_listings=True)
    assert sum(p.startswith("/listings/") for p in fetcher.paths) == 4
    assert report.listings_reason == "requested"


def test_failed_dataset_keeps_the_rest_and_waits_before_retrying():
    state = store.State()
    report, _ = run(state, NOV_9, {"/trading/corporate-actions/": FetchError("Gave up after 3 attempts")})
    assert report.exit_code == 1
    assert report.errors["corporate_actions"] == "FetchError: Gave up after 3 attempts"
    assert report.datasets["corporate_actions"] == "stale"
    assert state.status["corporate_actions"].stale is True
    assert state.status["quotes"].stale is False
    assert state.last_failure_at == NOV_9
    plan = refresh.due(state, NOV_9 + dt.timedelta(minutes=30), SETTINGS)
    assert plan.corporate_actions and not plan.quotes
    assert not plan.automatic_allowed(NOV_9 + dt.timedelta(minutes=30))
    report, fetcher = run(state, NOV_9 + dt.timedelta(minutes=60))
    assert [p.split("?")[0] for p in fetcher.paths] == ["/trading/corporate-actions/"]
    assert report.exit_code == 0 and state.last_failure_at is None


def test_failed_market_is_retried_alone():
    state = store.State()
    report, _ = run(state, NOV_9, {"/listings/listed-companies/?market=51": FetchError("boom")})
    assert report.errors["listings"] == "Bond Market: FetchError: boom"
    assert state.markets["Bond Market"].stale is True
    report, fetcher = run(state, NOV_10)
    assert [p for p in fetcher.paths if p.startswith("/listings/")] == ["/listings/listed-companies/?market=51"]
    assert report.listings_reason == "retrying failed market(s): Bond Market"
    assert report.exit_code == 0 and state.status["listings"].stale is False


def test_quotes_layout_change_is_an_error():
    state = store.State()
    run(state, NOV_9)
    renamed = fixture_html("trade-quotes.html").replace("Closing <br />Price <br />($)", "Close ($)").replace(
        "Last <br />Traded <br />Price <br />($)", "Last ($)")
    report, _ = run(state, NOV_10, {"/trading/trade-quotes/": renamed})
    assert report.exit_code == 1
    assert report.errors["quotes"].startswith("ParseError: trade-quotes: no quotes table")
    assert state.status["quotes"].stale is True


def test_holiday_counts_as_a_successful_fetch():
    state = store.State()
    run(state, NOV_9)
    report, _ = run(state, NOV_10, {"/trading/trade-quotes/": fixture_html("trade-quotes-holiday.html")})
    assert report.exit_code == 0
    assert state.status["quotes"].as_of == dt.date(2026, 11, 9)  # previous quotes kept
    assert state.status["quotes"].fetched_at == NOV_10
    assert state.companies["9001"].quote.date == dt.date(2026, 11, 9)
    assert not refresh.due(state, NOV_10 + dt.timedelta(hours=1), SETTINGS).anything


def test_three_trading_days_without_data_mark_quotes_stale_but_keep_trying():
    state = store.State()
    run(state, NOV_9)
    no_data = {"/trading/trade-quotes/": fixture_html("trade-quotes-holiday.html")}
    run(state, NOV_10, no_data)
    run(state, NOV_11, no_data)
    assert state.status["quotes"].no_data_days == 2 and state.status["quotes"].stale is False
    report, _ = run(state, NOV_12, no_data)
    message = "No trade data for 3 trading days in a row; the site may have changed"
    assert report.exit_code == 0 and message in report.warnings
    assert state.status["quotes"].stale is True and state.status["indices"].last_error == message
    assert state.last_failure_at is None and state.blocked_close is None  # not a failure, not a block
    plan = refresh.due(state, NOV_13, SETTINGS)
    assert plan.quotes and plan.automatic_allowed(NOV_13)
    run(state, NOV_13)
    assert state.status["quotes"].stale is False and state.status["quotes"].no_data_days == 0


def test_missing_optional_column_is_reported():
    no_volume = fixture_html("trade-quotes.html").replace("<th>Closing<br />Ask ($)</th><th>Volume</th>",
                                                         "<th>Closing<br />Ask ($)</th><th>Shares</th>")
    report, _ = run(store.State(), NOV_9, {"/trading/trade-quotes/": no_volume})
    assert report.exit_code == 0
    assert "trade-quotes: column(s) not found, values will be null: volume" in report.warnings
    assert "Warnings:" in report.text()


def test_corporate_actions_catch_up_after_an_outage():
    state = store.State()
    run(state, NOV_9)
    _, fetcher = run(state, DEC_24)  # 45 days later, longer than the 30-day window
    assert fetcher.paths[-1] == (
        "/trading/corporate-actions/?instrumentCode=all-stocks&fromDate=2026-10-10&thruDate=2027-12-24"
    )


def test_block_stops_all_requests_and_waits_for_the_next_close():
    state = store.State()
    report, fetcher = run(state, NOV_9, {"/trading/trade-quotes/": BlockedError("HTTP 403 for /x")})
    assert fetcher.request_count == 1
    assert report.blocked and report.exit_code == 1
    assert set(report.errors) == {"quotes", "indices", "listings", "corporate_actions"}
    assert all(s.stale for s in state.status.values())
    assert state.blocked_close == refresh.latest_close(NOV_9)
    assert refresh.BLOCKED_MESSAGE in report.text()
    assert not JSE_URL.search(report.text())
    plan = refresh.due(state, NOV_9 + dt.timedelta(hours=5), SETTINGS)
    assert not plan.automatic_allowed(NOV_9 + dt.timedelta(hours=5))
    report, _ = run(state, NOV_10)  # next close: tried again, and it works
    assert report.exit_code == 0 and state.blocked_close is None


def test_report_text():
    report, _ = run(store.State(), NOV_9, {"/trading/corporate-actions/": FetchError("boom")})
    text = report.text()
    assert text.startswith("JSE refresh for trading day 2026-11-09")
    assert "Requests to the JSE site: 6" in text
    assert "Datasets: corporate_actions stale, indices updated, listings updated, quotes updated" in text
    assert "Instruments: added 2, updated 0, delisted 0 (2 stored, 2 listed)" in text
    assert "Corporate actions added: 0 (0 stored)" in text
    assert "Errors:\n  - corporate_actions: FetchError: boom" in text
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Append the refresh run and report**

Append to the end of `jamstockex/refresh.py`:

```python


# --- one refresh run --------------------------------------------------------

BLOCKED_MESSAGE = (
    "The JSE site is blocking automated requests from this computer. Your stored data is still served; "
    "automatic refresh will try again after the next market close."
)


class _WarningCollector(logging.Handler):
    """Copies parser warnings (missing columns, skipped rows) into the report."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(logging.WARNING)
        self.messages: list[str] = []

    def emit(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> None:
        self.messages.append(record.getMessage())


@dataclass
class Report:
    trade_date: dt.date
    requests: int = 0
    datasets: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)  # dataset -> updated | stale | skipped
    errors: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    listings_reason: str | None = None
    stats: store.MergeStats = field(default_factory=store.MergeStats)
    counts: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    blocked: bool = False

    @property
    def attempted(self) -> bool:
        return any(outcome != "skipped" for outcome in self.datasets.values())

    @property
    def exit_code(self) -> int:
        return 1 if self.errors else 0

    def text(self) -> str:
        lines = [f"JSE refresh for trading day {self.trade_date.isoformat()}"]
        if not self.attempted:
            lines.append("Nothing is due: the stored data is up to date.")
            return "\n".join(lines)
        lines += [
            f"Requests to the JSE site: {self.requests}",
            "Datasets: " + ", ".join(f"{name} {outcome}" for name, outcome in sorted(self.datasets.items())),
            f"Listings refreshed: {self.listings_reason or 'no'}",
            f"Instruments: added {self.stats.companies_added}, updated {self.stats.companies_updated}, "
            f"delisted {self.stats.companies_delisted} ({self.counts.get('instruments', 0)} stored, "
            f"{self.counts.get('listed', 0)} listed)",
            f"Quotes in this refresh: {self.counts.get('quotes', 0)}",
            f"Corporate actions added: {self.stats.actions_added} ({self.counts.get('corporate_actions', 0)} stored)",
        ]
        if self.errors:
            lines += ["Errors:", *(f"  - {name}: {message}" for name, message in sorted(self.errors.items()))]
        if self.warnings:
            lines += ["Warnings:", *(f"  - {warning}" for warning in self.warnings)]
        if self.blocked:
            lines.append(BLOCKED_MESSAGE)
        return "\n".join(lines)


def _describe(exc: Exception) -> str:
    return f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"


def run_refresh(state: store.State, fetcher, now: dt.datetime, settings: Config,
                force_listings: bool = False) -> Report:
    """Fetch, parse and merge whatever is due now. Mutates `state`; the caller saves it.

    `fetcher` is a fetch.Fetcher (or a test double with `get`, `request_count`, `blocked`).
    """
    collector = _WarningCollector()
    parse_log = logging.getLogger("jamstockex.parse")
    parse_log.addHandler(collector)
    try:
        report = _run(state, fetcher, now, settings, force_listings)
    finally:
        parse_log.removeHandler(collector)
    report.warnings = collector.messages + report.warnings
    return report


def _run(state: store.State, fetcher, now: dt.datetime, settings: Config, force_listings: bool) -> Report:
    plan = due(state, now, settings, force_listings=force_listings)
    for label in set(state.markets) - set(settings.markets.values()):
        del state.markets[label]  # market removed from config.toml
    result = store.ScrapeResult(trade_date=plan.trade_date)

    # 1. Quotes first, so an unknown instrument can trigger a listings refresh in the same run.
    if plan.quotes:
        try:
            html = fetcher.get(QUOTES_PATH.format(market=settings.combined_market, date=plan.trade_date.isoformat()))
            result.quotes, result.indices = parse_trade_quotes(html, plan.trade_date)
        except (FetchError, ParseError) as exc:
            log.error("Trade quotes failed: %s", exc)
            result.errors["quotes"] = result.errors["indices"] = _describe(exc)

    # 2. Listings, only the markets that are due.
    codes, reason = listings_due(state, result.quotes, settings.markets, plan.today,
                                 settings.refresh.listings_every_days, force_listings)
    if codes:
        log.info("Refreshing listings for %d market(s): %s", len(codes), reason)
        result.listings = {}
        result.listings_full = len(codes) == len(settings.markets)
        for code in codes:
            label = settings.markets[code]
            try:
                html = fetcher.get(LISTINGS_PATH.format(market=code))
                result.listings[label] = parse_listed_companies(html, label)
            except (FetchError, ParseError) as exc:
                log.error("Listings for %s failed: %s", label, exc)
                result.listing_errors[label] = _describe(exc)
        if result.listing_errors:
            result.errors["listings"] = "; ".join(f"{label}: {e}" for label, e in result.listing_errors.items())

    # 3. Corporate actions: backfill until the first success, then from the last success minus the window.
    if plan.corporate_actions:
        from_date, thru_date = actions_window(state.status["corporate_actions"], plan.today, settings)
        try:
            html = fetcher.get(ACTIONS_PATH.format(from_date=from_date.isoformat(), thru_date=thru_date.isoformat()))
            result.actions = parse_corporate_actions(html)
        except (FetchError, ParseError) as exc:
            log.error("Corporate actions failed: %s", exc)
            result.errors["corporate_actions"] = _describe(exc)

    attempted = {
        "listings": result.listings is not None,
        "quotes": result.quotes is not None or "quotes" in result.errors,
        "indices": result.indices is not None or "indices" in result.errors,
        "corporate_actions": result.actions is not None or "corporate_actions" in result.errors,
    }
    stats = store.MergeStats()
    warnings = store.merge(state, result, today=plan.today, now=now, settings=settings, stats=stats)

    blocked = bool(getattr(fetcher, "blocked", False))
    if blocked:
        state.blocked_close = plan.close
    elif result.errors:
        state.blocked_close = None
        state.last_failure_at = now
    elif any(attempted.values()):
        state.blocked_close = None
        state.last_failure_at = None

    return Report(
        trade_date=plan.trade_date,
        requests=fetcher.request_count,
        datasets={
            name: "stale" if name in result.errors else ("updated" if done else "skipped")
            for name, done in attempted.items()
        },
        errors=dict(result.errors),
        warnings=warnings,
        listings_reason=reason,
        stats=stats,
        counts={
            "instruments": len(state.companies),
            "listed": sum(1 for c in state.companies.values() if c.listed),
            "quotes": len(result.quotes or {}),
            "corporate_actions": len(state.actions),
        },
        blocked=blocked,
    )
```

### Task 14: Docs assets: vendored Swagger UI, docs page and `openapi.json`

**Why:** `/docs` serves Swagger UI from files packaged inside `jamstockex/`, read with `importlib.resources`, so the docs work offline from the source tree, the `.pyz` and the PyInstaller build. The pinned `swagger-ui-dist` 5.33.3 files and their Apache-2.0 `LICENSE` and `NOTICE` are vendored into `jamstockex/static/swagger/`, with the version in `VERSION` (spec §5.5). The server (Task 15) serves these files; this task only adds them and their tests.

**Files:**
- Create: `jamstockex/static/swagger/{swagger-ui-bundle.js,swagger-ui.css,swagger-ui-bundle.js.LICENSE.txt,LICENSE,NOTICE,VERSION}` (vendored), `jamstockex/static/docs.html`, `jamstockex/static/docs-init.js`, `jamstockex/openapi.json`
- Test: `tests/test_static.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `tests.helpers.JSE_URL` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - Packaged resources (relative to `importlib.resources.files("jamstockex")`): `openapi.json`, `static/docs.html`, `static/docs-init.js`, `static/swagger/swagger-ui-bundle.js`, `static/swagger/swagger-ui.css`, plus the license files and `VERSION`
  - `openapi.json` paths, exactly: `/`, `/docs`, `/docs/assets/{file}`, `/openapi.json`, `/v2/`, `/v2/status`, `/v2/stocks`, `/v2/stocks/{instrument_code}`, `/v2/indices`, `/v2/corporate-actions`, `/v2/dividends`. Query parameters: `/v2/stocks` takes `symbol`, `market`, `listed`; `/v2/corporate-actions` takes `symbol`, `type`; `/v2/dividends` takes `symbol`, `upcoming`. Task 15's parity test checks these against the router.
  - `components.schemas.DatasetStatus`: `as_of`, `fetched_at`, `stale`, `last_error` required, plus the optional `no_data_days` (quotes only, Task 11). Examples use numeric `instrument_code` values such as `9001`.
  - `docs.html` loads `/docs/assets/swagger-ui.css`, `/docs/assets/swagger-ui-bundle.js` and `/docs/assets/docs-init.js`; `docs-init.js` points Swagger UI at `/openapi.json` with `validatorUrl: null`

`tests/test_static.py`:

```python
"""The packaged docs files: vendored Swagger UI, the docs page and openapi.json."""

import hashlib
import importlib.resources
import json
import re

import pytest

from tests.helpers import JSE_URL

PACKAGE = importlib.resources.files("jamstockex")
SWAGGER_VERSION = "5.33.3"
VENDORED_SHA256 = {
    "swagger-ui-bundle.js": "3861ec4cc8e7b9fa9f250280da9350436e418137788282c753b9a2ac5fb7bd2f",
    "swagger-ui.css": "1ac324f7dcd27e4b9386b4bd6421271ec147e922a22c05ba24b11515e9aa6321",
    "swagger-ui-bundle.js.LICENSE.txt": "e5a4792feaa5332dfba37f1d7adf84308b93b4f2d8da0569a9f48a3cb61c969b",
    "LICENSE": "cfc7749b96f63bd31c3c42b5c471bf756814053e847c10f3eb003417bc523d30",
    "NOTICE": "0d20d1adef18aee3f40dd258172155521ce702ac445cb5f7b7d60ed32dad2fb2",
}


def read(name):
    return PACKAGE.joinpath(name).read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def openapi():
    return json.loads(read("openapi.json"))


def test_swagger_version_is_recorded():
    assert read("static/swagger/VERSION") == SWAGGER_VERSION + "\n"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name, digest", VENDORED_SHA256.items())
def test_vendored_files_are_the_pinned_release(name, digest):
    data = PACKAGE.joinpath("static/swagger", name).read_bytes()
    assert hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() == digest


def test_swagger_license_is_apache_2():
    assert "Apache License" in read("static/swagger/LICENSE")
    assert "Version 2.0" in read("static/swagger/LICENSE")


def test_docs_page_loads_local_assets_without_inline_script():
    page = read("static/docs.html")
    assert '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/docs/assets/swagger-ui.css">' in page
    scripts = re.findall(r"<script[^>]*>", page)
    assert scripts == ['<script src="/docs/assets/swagger-ui-bundle.js">', '<script src="/docs/assets/docs-init.js">']
    assert "http://" not in page and "https://" not in page


def test_docs_init_script_points_at_the_local_openapi_document():
    script = read("static/docs-init.js")
    assert 'url: "/openapi.json"' in script
    assert "validatorUrl: null" in script  # no badge image from an outside validator


def test_openapi_document_shape():
    doc = openapi()
    assert doc["openapi"] == "3.1.0"
    assert doc["info"]["version"] == "2.0.0"
    assert doc["servers"] == [{"url": "/", "description": "This app, on 127.0.0.1"}]
    for path, item in doc["paths"].items():
        assert set(item) == {"get"}, path
        responses = item["get"]["responses"]
        assert {"403", "405", "500"} <= set(responses), path
        ok = responses.get("200") or responses.get("302")
        assert ok, path


def test_every_v2_json_response_has_an_example():
    for path, item in openapi()["paths"].items():
        if path.startswith("/v2/"):
            body = item["get"]["responses"]["200"]["content"]["application/json"]
            assert body["example"]["meta"]["api_version"] == "v2", path


def test_every_ref_resolves():
    doc = openapi()
    refs = set(re.findall(r'"\$ref": "#/components/(\w+)/(\w+)"', read("openapi.json")))
    assert refs
    assert {(kind, name) for kind, name in refs if name not in doc["components"][kind]} == set()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["openapi.json", "static/docs.html", "static/docs-init.js"])
def test_no_jse_links(name):
    assert not JSE_URL.search(read(name))
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Vendor Swagger UI and write the docs files**

First vendor the pinned Swagger UI release. The tarball comes from the npm registry (not the JSE site) and must match the SHA-256 recorded here (computed 2026-11-10 from `swagger-ui-dist-5.33.3.tgz`; it also matches the registry's published sha512 integrity). **If the checksum line doesn't print `OK`, stop and tell the user; don't use the files.**

```bash
SWAGGER_VERSION=5.33.3
DL="$(mktemp -d)"
curl -fsSL -o "$DL/swagger-ui-dist.tgz" "https://registry.npmjs.org/swagger-ui-dist/-/swagger-ui-dist-$SWAGGER_VERSION.tgz"
echo "43b39460ac8d3136c88b939ebaf70e253284c2a6b2b535fb3b41d0066c4fce72  $DL/swagger-ui-dist.tgz" | shasum -a 256 -c - || exit 1
mkdir "$DL/x" && tar -xzf "$DL/swagger-ui-dist.tgz" -C "$DL/x"
mkdir -p jamstockex/static/swagger
for f in swagger-ui-bundle.js swagger-ui.css swagger-ui-bundle.js.LICENSE.txt LICENSE NOTICE; do cp "$DL/x/package/$f" jamstockex/static/swagger/; done
printf '%s\n' "$SWAGGER_VERSION" > jamstockex/static/swagger/VERSION
rm -rf "$DL"
(cd jamstockex/static/swagger && shasum -a 256 swagger-ui-bundle.js swagger-ui.css swagger-ui-bundle.js.LICENSE.txt LICENSE NOTICE)
```

Expected: `…/swagger-ui-dist.tgz: OK`, then exactly these five hashes (the same ones `tests/test_static.py` pins):

```text
3861ec4cc8e7b9fa9f250280da9350436e418137788282c753b9a2ac5fb7bd2f  swagger-ui-bundle.js
1ac324f7dcd27e4b9386b4bd6421271ec147e922a22c05ba24b11515e9aa6321  swagger-ui.css
e5a4792feaa5332dfba37f1d7adf84308b93b4f2d8da0569a9f48a3cb61c969b  swagger-ui-bundle.js.LICENSE.txt
cfc7749b96f63bd31c3c42b5c471bf756814053e847c10f3eb003417bc523d30  LICENSE
0d20d1adef18aee3f40dd258172155521ce702ac445cb5f7b7d60ed32dad2fb2  NOTICE
```

Then write the docs page, its init script (a separate file, so the CSP needs no inline scripts) and the hand-written OpenAPI document. `openapi.json` describes every route, filter, response envelope and error, with an example for every `/v2/` response; no URL in it points at the JSE site.

`jamstockex/static/docs.html`:

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <title>JSE data API (local) - docs</title>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/docs/assets/swagger-ui.css">
</head>
<body>
  <div id="swagger-ui"></div>
  <script src="/docs/assets/swagger-ui-bundle.js"></script>
  <script src="/docs/assets/docs-init.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

```javascript
// Starts Swagger UI on /docs. A separate file, so the page's CSP needs no inline scripts.
window.addEventListener("load", function () {
  window.ui = SwaggerUIBundle({
    url: "/openapi.json",
    dom_id: "#swagger-ui",
    deepLinking: true,
    validatorUrl: null
  });
});
```

```json
          "change": {"type": ["number", "null"]},
          "change_pct": {"type": ["number", "null"]},
          "volume": {"type": ["integer", "null"]}
        },
        "title": "MarketIndex"
      },
      "DatasetStatus": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["as_of", "fetched_at", "stale", "last_error"],
        "properties": {
          "as_of": {"type": ["string", "null"], "format": "date"},
          "fetched_at": {"type": ["string", "null"], "format": "date-time"},
          "stale": {"type": "boolean"},
          "last_error": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
          "no_data_days": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "description": "Quotes only, present when above 0: trading-day refreshes in a row whose page had no trade data. At 3, quotes and indices are marked stale."}
        }
      },
      "Status": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": [
          "listings",
          "quotes",
          "indices",
          "corporate_actions",
          "refreshing",
          "blocked",
          "next_check_at",
          "data_file"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "listings": {
            "allOf": [
              {"$ref": "#/components/schemas/DatasetStatus"},
              {
                "type": "object",
                "required": ["markets", "unmatched_codes"],
                "properties": {
                  "markets": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "additionalProperties": {"$ref": "#/components/schemas/DatasetStatus"}
                  },
                  "unmatched_codes": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "items": {"type": "string"},
                    "description": "Quote codes that no listings page explained"
                  }
                }
              }
            ]
          },
          "quotes": {"$ref": "#/components/schemas/DatasetStatus"},
          "indices": {"$ref": "#/components/schemas/DatasetStatus"},
          "corporate_actions": {"$ref": "#/components/schemas/DatasetStatus"},
          "refreshing": {"type": "boolean"},
          "blocked": {"type": "boolean", "description": "The JSE site blocked the last automated request"},
          "next_check_at": {"type": ["string", "null"], "format": "date-time"},
          "data_file": {"type": "string"}
        }
      },
      "ApiIndex": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["name", "api_version", "endpoints", "notice", "source", "license"],
        "properties": {
          "name": {"type": "string"},
          "api_version": {"type": "string"},
          "endpoints": {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": {"type": "string"}},
          "notice": {"type": "string"},
          "source": {"type": "string"},
          "license": {"type": "string"}
        }
      },
      "Error": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["error"],
        "properties": {
          "error": {
            "type": "object",
            "required": ["status", "message"],
            "properties": {"status": {"type": "integer"}, "message": {"type": "string"}}
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "responses": {
      "BadRequest": {
        "description": "Unknown filter, repeated filter or invalid boolean",
        "content": {
          "application/json": {
            "schema": {"$ref": "#/components/schemas/Error"},
            "example": {
              "error": {"status": 400, "message": "Unknown filter(s): sort; allowed: symbol, market, listed"}
            }
          }
        }
      },
      "NotFound": {
        "description": "No such endpoint, instrument or asset",
        "content": {
          "application/json": {
            "schema": {"$ref": "#/components/schemas/Error"},
            "example": {"error": {"status": 404, "message": "Unknown instrument_code: 9099"}}
          }
        }
      },
      "MethodNotAllowed": {
        "description": "Only GET is allowed",
        "content": {
          "application/json": {
            "schema": {"$ref": "#/components/schemas/Error"},
            "example": {"error": {"status": 405, "message": "Method POST not allowed; this API is read-only"}}
          }
        },
        "headers": {"Allow": {"schema": {"type": "string", "example": "GET"}}}
      },
      "ServerError": {
        "description": "Unexpected error; details go to the console only",
        "content": {
          "application/json": {
            "schema": {"$ref": "#/components/schemas/Error"},
            "example": {"error": {"status": 500, "message": "Internal server error"}}
          }
        }
      },
      "Forbidden": {
        "description": "The Host header is not 127.0.0.1:<port> or localhost:<port>",
        "content": {
          "text/plain": {
            "schema": {"type": "string"},
            "example": "Forbidden: this API only answers requests addressed to 127.0.0.1:8765 or localhost:8765.\n"
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

---

### Task 15: HTTP server

**Why:** serves the read-only API and the docs from an immutable `Snapshot`, on `127.0.0.1` only (spec §5):
- **Routes:** `/` (302 → `/docs`), `/docs`, `/docs/assets/<file>`, `/openapi.json`, `/v2/`, `/v2/status`, `/v2/stocks`, `/v2/stocks/{instrument_code}`, `/v2/indices`, `/v2/corporate-actions`, `/v2/dividends`.
- **Filters** are exact and case-insensitive. An unknown or repeated filter, or an invalid boolean, gets 400. Stocks are sorted by `instrument_code`, actions by `record_date` then `symbol`.
- **Errors:** 400/404/405 JSON (405 with `Allow: GET`); an unhandled exception gets a generic 500, and the traceback goes to the console only.
- **Local-only protections:** the `Host` header must be `127.0.0.1:<port>` or `localhost:<port>` (else 403, plain text); no CORS headers; `nosniff` and `no-store` on every response; the docs CSP on `/docs`; minified, sorted UTF-8 JSON.
- **Parity:** every route the router answers is in `openapi.json`, with the same query parameters, and every documented path is answered.

**Files:**
- Create: `jamstockex/server.py`
- Test: `tests/test_server.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `jamstockex.store.State`, `DatasetStatus`, `action_sort_key`, `utc_timestamp` (Task 10); `jamstockex.config.JAMAICA` (Task 2); the packaged docs files and `openapi.json` (Task 14); `tests.helpers.JSE_URL` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - Constants `API_VERSION = "v2"`, `SOURCE`, `NOTICE`, `LICENSE`, `DOCS_CSP`; `ROUTES: tuple[str, ...]` (the eleven paths); `FILTERS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]`; `ASSETS: dict[str, tuple[str, str]]`
  - `resource(name: str) -> bytes` (cached `importlib.resources` read); `encode_json(doc: Any) -> bytes`
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) Snapshot(stocks, actions, indices, status, stock_meta, saved_at, data_file, blocked, refreshing=False, next_check_at=None)` with `@classmethod from_state(cls, state: State, *, data_file: str) -> Snapshot` and `with_runtime(self, *, refreshing: bool, next_check_at: datetime | None) -> Snapshot`
  - `match_route(path: str) -> tuple[str, dict[str, str]] | None`
  - `class LocalServer(ThreadingHTTPServer)` with `snapshot_provider`, `today` and the property `port -> int`
  - `make_server(port: int, snapshot_provider: Callable[[], Snapshot], *, today: Callable[[], date] | None = None) -> LocalServer`: binds `("127.0.0.1", port)` (`0` picks a free port) and raises `OSError` if the port is in use. There is no host parameter.

```python
import datetime as dt
import http.client
import importlib.resources
import inspect
import json
import threading
from decimal import Decimal

import pytest

from jamstockex import server, store
from jamstockex.models import Company, CorporateAction, Index, Quote
from tests.helpers import JSE_URL

DAY = dt.date(2026, 11, 9)
NOW = dt.datetime(2026, 11, 9, 22, 31, 4, tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)
TODAY = dt.date(2026, 11, 25)
META_KEYS = {"api_version", "as_of", "saved_at", "stale", "source", "notice", "license"}


def action(symbol, code, type_="dividend", record=None, payment=None, amount="0.75", currency="JMD"):
    return CorporateAction(symbol=symbol, type=type_, amount=Decimal(amount) if amount else None,
                           currency=currency, record_date=record, ex_date=None, payment_date=payment,
                           instrument_code=code)


def sample_state():
    state = store.State(saved_at=NOW)
    quote = Quote(date=DAY, close=Decimal("12.50"), change=Decimal("-0.25"), change_pct=Decimal("-1.96"),
                  volume=48213)
    state.companies = {
        "9001": Company(instrument_code="9001", symbol="ACME", name="Acme Holdings Limited",
                            market="Main Market", currency="JMD", website="https://www.acme.example",
                            listed_since=DAY, quote=quote),
        "9002": Company(instrument_code="9002", symbol="BETA", name="Beta Capital Limited",
                            market="USD Market", currency="USD", listed_since=DAY),
        "9005": Company(instrument_code="9005", symbol="OLD", name="Old Co", market="Main Market",
                           listed=False, delisted_on=DAY),
    }
    state.indices = [Index(name="JSE Main Index", date=DAY, value=Decimal("325456.78"), change=None,
                           change_pct=None, volume=None)]
    state.actions = [
        action("BETA", "9002", record=dt.date(2026, 11, 20), payment=dt.date(2026, 12, 4), currency="USD"),
        action("ACME", "9001", record=dt.date(2026, 11, 20), payment=dt.date(2026, 11, 24)),
        action("ACME", "9001", type_="stock_split", record=dt.date(2026, 4, 14), amount=None),
        action("ZZZ", None, record=dt.date(2026, 5, 1), payment=dt.date(2026, 5, 15)),
    ]
    fresh = store.DatasetStatus(as_of=DAY, fetched_at=NOW)
    state.status = {"listings": store.DatasetStatus(as_of=DAY, fetched_at=NOW, stale=True,
                                                    last_error="USD Market: FetchError: x"),
                    "quotes": fresh, "indices": fresh,
                    "corporate_actions": store.DatasetStatus(as_of=DAY, fetched_at=NOW)}
    state.markets = {"Main Market": store.DatasetStatus(as_of=DAY, fetched_at=NOW),
                     "USD Market": store.DatasetStatus(as_of=DAY, stale=True, last_error="FetchError: x")}
    state.unmatched = {"9003"}
    return state


class Holder:
    def __init__(self, snapshot):
        self.snapshot = snapshot

    def __call__(self):
        return self.snapshot


@pytest.fixture
def api():
    holder = Holder(server.Snapshot.from_state(sample_state(), data_file="/data/jse-data.json"))
    srv = server.make_server(0, holder, today=lambda: TODAY)
    thread = threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, kwargs={"poll_interval": 0.05}, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    yield srv, holder
    srv.shutdown()
    srv.server_close()


def request(srv, path, method="GET", host=None, headers=None):
    conn = http.client.HTTPConnection("127.0.0.1", srv.port, timeout=5)
    all_headers = {"Host": host or f"127.0.0.1:{srv.port}", **(headers or {})}
    conn.request(method, path, headers=all_headers)
    response = conn.getresponse()
    body = response.read()
    conn.close()
    return response, body


def get_json(srv, path):
    response, body = request(srv, path)
    return response.status, json.loads(body), response


def test_binds_127_0_0_1_only_and_has_no_host_option(api):
    srv, _ = api
    assert srv.server_address[0] == "127.0.0.1"
    assert "host" not in inspect.signature(server.make_server).parameters


def test_status(api):
    srv, _ = api
    status, doc, _ = get_json(srv, "/v2/status")
    assert status == 200
    assert set(doc) == {"meta", "data"} and set(doc["meta"]) == META_KEYS
    data = doc["data"]
    assert set(data) == {"listings", "quotes", "indices", "corporate_actions", "refreshing", "blocked",
                         "next_check_at", "data_file"}
    assert data["quotes"] == {"as_of": "2026-11-09", "fetched_at": "2026-11-09T22:31:04Z", "stale": False,
                              "last_error": None}
    assert data["listings"]["markets"]["USD Market"]["last_error"] == "FetchError: x"
    assert data["listings"]["unmatched_codes"] == ["9003"]
    assert data["refreshing"] is False and data["blocked"] is False and data["next_check_at"] is None
    assert data["data_file"] == "/data/jse-data.json"
    assert doc["meta"]["stale"] is True  # listings is stale
    assert doc["meta"]["saved_at"] == "2026-11-09T22:31:04Z"


def test_index_lists_endpoints(api):
    status, doc, _ = get_json(api[0], "/v2/")
    assert status == 200
    assert doc["data"]["endpoints"]["stock"] == "/v2/stocks/{instrument_code}"


def test_stocks_sorted_with_quotes_and_meta(api):
    status, doc, _ = get_json(api[0], "/v2/stocks")
    assert status == 200
    assert [s["instrument_code"] for s in doc["data"]] == ["9001", "9002", "9005"]
    assert doc["data"][0]["quote"] == {"date": "2026-11-09", "close": 12.5, "change": -0.25,
                                       "change_pct": -1.96, "volume": 48213}
    assert doc["meta"]["as_of"] == "2026-11-09" and doc["meta"]["stale"] is True


@pytest.mark.parametrize("query, codes", [
    ("symbol=acme", ["9001"]),
    ("market=usd%20market", ["9002"]),
    ("listed=true", ["9001", "9002"]),
    ("listed=FALSE", ["9005"]),
    ("market=Main+Market&listed=true", ["9001"]),
    ("symbol=nope", []),
])
def test_stock_filters(api, query, codes):

def test_one_stock_with_actions_and_per_market_stale(api):
    status, doc, _ = get_json(api[0], "/v2/stocks/9001")
    assert status == 200
    assert [a["type"] for a in doc["data"]["corporate_actions"]] == ["stock_split", "dividend"]
    assert [a["type"] for a in doc["data"]["dividends"]] == ["dividend"]
    assert doc["meta"] == {**doc["meta"], "as_of": "2026-11-09", "stale": False}  # Main Market is fine
    _, beta, _ = get_json(api[0], "/v2/stocks/9002")
    assert beta["meta"]["stale"] is True  # USD Market failed
    assert beta["meta"]["as_of"] == "2026-11-09"  # no quote: its market's as_of

def test_corporate_actions_sorted_by_record_date_then_symbol(api):

@pytest.mark.parametrize("path, symbols", [
    ("/v2/corporate-actions?type=STOCK_SPLIT", ["ACME"]),

def test_action_filters(api, path, symbols):

@pytest.mark.parametrize("path, message", [
    ("/v2/stocks?sort=name", "Unknown filter(s): sort; allowed: symbol, market, listed"),
    ("/v2/status?x=1", "Unknown filter(s): x; this endpoint takes none"),
    ("/v2/stocks?listed=yes", "Invalid value for listed: 'yes' (use true or false)"),

    ("/v2/stocks?symbol=A&symbol=B", "Filter(s) given more than once: symbol"),
])
def test_bad_requests(api, path, message):

@pytest.mark.parametrize("method", ["POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "PATCH", "OPTIONS", "HEAD"])
def test_other_methods_are_405_with_allow_get(api, method):
    response, body = request(api[0], "/v2/stocks", method=method)
    assert response.status == 405
    assert response.getheader("Allow") == "GET"

        assert json.loads(body)["error"]["status"] == 405


def test_unhandled_error_is_a_generic_500(api, caplog):
    srv, holder = api

    def broken():
        raise RuntimeError("secret detail")

    srv.snapshot_provider = broken
    status, doc, _ = get_json(srv, "/v2/stocks")
    assert status == 500
    assert doc == {"error": {"status": 500, "message": "Internal server error"}}
    assert "RuntimeError: secret detail" in caplog.text  # traceback on the console only

    assert response.getheader("Content-Type") == "text/plain; charset=utf-8"
    assert b"127.0.0.1:" in body

    conn.endheaders()
    assert conn.getresponse().status == 403

    assert response.getheader("Cache-Control") == "no-store"

    assert response.getheader("Cache-Control") == "no-store"

    ("swagger-ui-bundle.js", "text/javascript; charset=utf-8", "static/swagger/swagger-ui-bundle.js"),
    ("swagger-ui.css", "text/css; charset=utf-8", "static/swagger/swagger-ui.css"),
    ("docs-init.js", "text/javascript; charset=utf-8", "static/docs-init.js"),
])
def test_docs_assets_are_served(api, name, content_type, resource):
    response, body = request(api[0], f"/docs/assets/{name}")
    assert response.status == 200
    assert response.getheader("Content-Type") == content_type
    assert body == importlib.resources.files("jamstockex").joinpath(resource).read_bytes()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["LICENSE", "..%2Fopenapi.json", "nope.js"])
def test_other_assets_are_404(api, name):
    response, _ = request(api[0], f"/docs/assets/{name}")
    assert response.status == 404


def test_root_redirects_to_docs(api):
    response, _ = request(api[0], "/")
    assert response.status == 302 and response.getheader("Location") == "/docs"


def test_openapi_json_is_served(api):
    response, body = request(api[0], "/openapi.json")
    assert response.status == 200
    assert response.getheader("Content-Type") == "application/json; charset=utf-8"
    assert body == importlib.resources.files("jamstockex").joinpath("openapi.json").read_bytes()


def test_json_is_minified_sorted_utf8(api):
    response, body = request(api[0], "/v2/stocks")
    assert response.getheader("Content-Type") == "application/json; charset=utf-8"
    doc = json.loads(body)
    assert body == json.dumps(doc, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")


def test_snapshot_swap_while_serving(api):
    srv, holder = api
    state = sample_state()
    del state.companies["9002"]
    state.blocked_close = NOW
    holder.snapshot = server.Snapshot.from_state(state, data_file="/data/jse-data.json").with_runtime(
        refreshing=True, next_check_at=NOW)
    _, doc, _ = get_json(srv, "/v2/stocks")
    assert [s["instrument_code"] for s in doc["data"]] == ["9001", "9005"]
    _, status, _ = get_json(srv, "/v2/status")
    assert status["data"]["refreshing"] is True and status["data"]["blocked"] is True
    assert status["data"]["next_check_at"] == "2026-11-09T22:31:04Z"


def test_empty_state_is_served(api):
    srv, holder = api
    holder.snapshot = server.Snapshot.from_state(store.State(), data_file="x")
    _, doc, _ = get_json(srv, "/v2/stocks")
    assert doc["data"] == [] and doc["meta"]["as_of"] is None and doc["meta"]["saved_at"] is None


def example_path(path):
    return path.replace("{instrument_code}", "9001").replace("{file}", "swagger-ui.css")


def test_every_route_is_documented_and_every_documented_path_is_answered(api):
    openapi = json.loads(importlib.resources.files("jamstockex").joinpath("openapi.json").read_text("utf-8"))
    assert set(openapi["paths"]) == set(server.ROUTES)
    for path, item in openapi["paths"].items():
        documented = {p["name"] for p in item["get"].get("parameters", []) if p["in"] == "query"}
        assert documented == set(server.FILTERS.get(path, ())), path
        response, _ = request(api[0], example_path(path))
        assert response.status in (200, 302), path


def test_no_jse_links_in_any_response(api):
    for path in server.ROUTES:
        if path.startswith("/v2/"):
            _, body = request(api[0], example_path(path))
            assert not JSE_URL.search(body.decode("utf-8")), path
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the server**

`jamstockex/server.py`:

```python
"""Read-only JSON API and Swagger UI docs, on http.server, for this computer only.

- Binds 127.0.0.1 (hard-coded; there is no host option) and answers only requests
  whose Host header is 127.0.0.1:<port> or localhost:<port>, which stops DNS rebinding.
- No CORS headers, so other browser origins can't read responses.
- Every response has X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff and Cache-Control: no-store.
- Responses come from an immutable Snapshot; the app swaps in a new one after each save.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt
import functools
import importlib.resources
import json
import logging
import socketserver
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from typing import Any, Callable
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, unquote, urlsplit

from jamstockex.config import JAMAICA
from jamstockex.store import State, action_sort_key, utc_timestamp

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

API_VERSION = "v2"
SOURCE = ("Data: Jamaica Stock Exchange Limited (jamstockex.com). "
          "All rights in the data belong to the JSE Group.")
NOTICE = "Personal, noncommercial use only. Not financial advice."
LICENSE = "Code: PolyForm Noncommercial 1.0.0"
DOCS_CSP = "default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'"

# Every path the router answers. tests/test_server.py checks this against openapi.json.
ROUTES = (
    "/", "/docs", "/docs/assets/{file}", "/openapi.json",
    "/v2/", "/v2/status", "/v2/stocks", "/v2/stocks/{instrument_code}",
    "/v2/indices", "/v2/corporate-actions", "/v2/dividends",
)
FILTERS = {
    "/v2/stocks": ("symbol", "market", "listed"),
    "/v2/corporate-actions": ("symbol", "type"),
    "/v2/dividends": ("symbol", "upcoming"),
}
ASSETS = {
    "swagger-ui-bundle.js": ("static/swagger/swagger-ui-bundle.js", "text/javascript; charset=utf-8"),
    "swagger-ui.css": ("static/swagger/swagger-ui.css", "text/css; charset=utf-8"),
    "docs-init.js": ("static/docs-init.js", "text/javascript; charset=utf-8"),
}
ENDPOINTS = {
    "status": "/v2/status",
    "stocks": "/v2/stocks",
    "stock": "/v2/stocks/{instrument_code}",
    "indices": "/v2/indices",
    "corporate_actions": "/v2/corporate-actions",
    "dividends": "/v2/dividends",
    "docs": "/docs",
    "openapi": "/openapi.json",
}


@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=None)
def resource(name: str) -> bytes:
    """A packaged file; works from the source tree, a .pyz and a PyInstaller build."""
    return importlib.resources.files("jamstockex").joinpath(name).read_bytes()


def encode_json(doc: Any) -> bytes:
    return json.dumps(doc, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")


# --- snapshot ------------------------------------------------------------------


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Snapshot:
    """Everything the API serves, precomputed from one saved State. Treat as read-only."""

    stocks: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]               # sorted by instrument_code, each with "quote"
    actions: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]              # sorted by record_date, then symbol
    indices: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]
    status: dict[str, Any]                           # the four datasets, as /v2/status shows them
    stock_meta: dict[str, tuple[str | None, bool]]   # instrument_code -> (as_of, stale)
    saved_at: str | None
    data_file: str
    blocked: bool
    refreshing: bool = False
    next_check_at: str | None = None

    @classmethod
    def from_state(cls, state: State, *, data_file: str) -> Snapshot:
        companies = sorted(state.companies.values(), key=lambda c: c.instrument_code)
        status = {name: s.to_json() for name, s in state.status.items()}
        status["listings"]["markets"] = {label: m.to_json() for label, m in sorted(state.markets.items())}
        status["listings"]["unmatched_codes"] = sorted(state.unmatched)
        stock_meta = {}
        for company in companies:
            market = state.markets.get(company.market, state.status["listings"])
            as_of = company.quote.date if company.quote else market.as_of
            stock_meta[company.instrument_code] = (
                as_of.isoformat() if as_of else None,
                state.status["quotes"].stale or market.stale,
            )
        return cls(
            stocks=tuple(c.to_json() for c in companies),
            actions=tuple(a.to_json() for a in sorted(state.actions, key=action_sort_key)),
            indices=tuple(i.to_json() for i in sorted(state.indices, key=lambda i: i.name)),
            status=status,
            stock_meta=stock_meta,
            saved_at=utc_timestamp(state.saved_at) if state.saved_at else None,
            data_file=data_file,
            blocked=state.blocked_close is not None,
        )

    def with_runtime(self, *, refreshing: bool, next_check_at: dt.datetime | None) -> Snapshot:
        return replace(self, refreshing=refreshing,
                       next_check_at=utc_timestamp(next_check_at) if next_check_at else None)


# --- HTTP ------------------------------------------------------------------------


class HttpError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, status: int, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status = status
        self.message = message


def match_route(path: str) -> tuple[str, dict[str, str]] | None:
    if path in ROUTES and "{" not in path:
        return path, {}
    for prefix, template, name in (("/v2/stocks/", "/v2/stocks/{instrument_code}", "instrument_code"),
                                   ("/docs/assets/", "/docs/assets/{file}", "file")):
        rest = path[len(prefix):]
        if path.startswith(prefix) and rest and "/" not in rest:
            return template, {name: unquote(rest)}
    return None


def _bool(name: str, value: str) -> bool:
    lowered = value.lower()
    if lowered not in ("true", "false"):
        raise HttpError(400, f"Invalid value for {name}: {value!r} (use true or false)")
    return lowered == "true"


def _same(a: Any, b: str) -> bool:
    return isinstance(a, str) and a.casefold() == b.casefold()


class LocalServer(ThreadingHTTPServer):
    daemon_threads = True
    # On Windows SO_REUSEADDR would let a second server bind a port that is already in use.
    allow_reuse_address = sys.platform != "win32"

    def __init__(self, port: int, snapshot_provider: Callable[[], Snapshot],
                 today: Callable[[], dt.date]) -> None:
        self.snapshot_provider = snapshot_provider
        self.today = today
        super().__init__(("127.0.0.1", port), _Handler)

    def server_bind(self) -> None:
        socketserver.TCPServer.server_bind(self)  # skip HTTPServer's reverse DNS lookup
        self.server_name = "127.0.0.1"
        self.server_port = self.server_address[1]

    @property
    def port(self) -> int:
        return self.server_address[1]


def make_server(port: int, snapshot_provider: Callable[[], Snapshot], *,
                today: Callable[[], dt.date] | None = None) -> LocalServer:
    """A server bound to 127.0.0.1:<port> (0 picks a free port). Raises OSError if the port is in use."""
    return LocalServer(port, snapshot_provider, today or (lambda: dt.datetime.now(JAMAICA).date()))


class _Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    server: LocalServer
    server_version = "jamstockex"
    sys_version = ""

    def log_message(self, format: str, *args: Any) -> None:
        log.debug("%s - %s", self.address_string(), format % args)

    # -- responses --

    def _send(self, status: int, body: bytes, content_type: str, extra: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> None:
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", content_type)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        for name, value in (extra or {}).items():
            self.send_header(name, value)
        self.end_headers()
        if self.command != "HEAD":
            self.wfile.write(body)

    def _json(self, status: int, doc: Any, extra: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> None:
        self._send(status, encode_json(doc), "application/json; charset=utf-8", extra)

    def _error(self, status: int, message: str, extra: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> None:
        self._json(status, {"error": {"status": status, "message": message}}, extra)

    def _host_ok(self) -> bool:
        port = self.server.port
        if self.headers.get("Host") in (f"127.0.0.1:{port}", f"localhost:{port}"):
            return True
        body = (f"Forbidden: this API only answers requests addressed to 127.0.0.1:{port} "
                f"or localhost:{port}.\n").encode("utf-8")
        self._send(403, body, "text/plain; charset=utf-8")
        return False

    # -- methods --

    def do_GET(self) -> None:
        if not self._host_ok():
            return
        try:
            self._route()
        except HttpError as exc:
            self._error(exc.status, exc.message)
        except Exception:
            log.exception("Unhandled error for GET %s", self.path)
            self._error(500, "Internal server error")

    def _not_allowed(self) -> None:
        if self._host_ok():
            self._error(405, f"Method {self.command} not allowed; this API is read-only", {"Allow": "GET"})

    do_HEAD = do_POST = do_PUT = do_PATCH = do_DELETE = do_OPTIONS = _not_allowed

    # -- routing --

    def _route(self) -> None:
        parts = urlsplit(self.path)
        matched = match_route(parts.path)
        if matched is None:
            raise HttpError(404, f"No such endpoint: {parts.path}")
        route, params = matched
        query = parse_qs(parts.query, keep_blank_values=True)
        allowed = FILTERS.get(route, ())
        unknown = sorted(set(query) - set(allowed))
        if unknown:
            hint = f"; allowed: {', '.join(allowed)}" if allowed else "; this endpoint takes none"
            raise HttpError(400, f"Unknown filter(s): {', '.join(unknown)}{hint}")
        repeated = sorted(name for name, values in query.items() if len(values) > 1)
        if repeated:
            raise HttpError(400, f"Filter(s) given more than once: {', '.join(repeated)}")
        filters = {name: values[0] for name, values in query.items()}

        if route == "/":
            self._send(302, b"", "text/plain; charset=utf-8", {"Location": "/docs"})
        elif route == "/docs":
            self._send(200, resource("static/docs.html"), "text/html; charset=utf-8",
                       {"Content-Security-Policy": DOCS_CSP})
        elif route == "/docs/assets/{file}":
            if params["file"] not in ASSETS:
                raise HttpError(404, f"No such asset: {params['file']}")
            name, content_type = ASSETS[params["file"]]
            self._send(200, resource(name), content_type)
        elif route == "/openapi.json":
            self._send(200, resource("openapi.json"), "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        else:
            self._api(route, params, filters, self.server.snapshot_provider())

    def _envelope(self, snap: Snapshot, as_of: str | None, stale: bool, data: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "meta": {
                "api_version": API_VERSION,
                "as_of": as_of,
                "saved_at": snap.saved_at,
                "stale": stale,
                "source": SOURCE,
                "notice": NOTICE,
                "license": LICENSE,
            },
            "data": data,
        }

    def _api(self, route: str, params: dict[str, str], filters: dict[str, str], snap: Snapshot) -> None:
        status = snap.status

        def dataset(name: str) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
            return status[name]["as_of"], status[name]["stale"]

        stocks_meta = (status["quotes"]["as_of"] or status["listings"]["as_of"],
                       status["quotes"]["stale"] or status["listings"]["stale"])
        overall = (stocks_meta[0], any(status[name]["stale"] for name in ("listings", "quotes", "indices",
                                                                         "corporate_actions")))
        if route == "/v2/":
            data = {"name": "jamstockex", "api_version": API_VERSION, "endpoints": ENDPOINTS,
                    "notice": NOTICE, "source": SOURCE, "license": LICENSE}
            doc = self._envelope(snap, *overall, data)
        elif route == "/v2/status":
            data = {**status, "refreshing": snap.refreshing, "blocked": snap.blocked,
                    "next_check_at": snap.next_check_at, "data_file": snap.data_file}
            doc = self._envelope(snap, *overall, data)
        elif route == "/v2/stocks":
            stocks = list(snap.stocks)
            if "symbol" in filters:
                stocks = [s for s in stocks if _same(s["symbol"], filters["symbol"])]
            if "market" in filters:
                stocks = [s for s in stocks if _same(s["market"], filters["market"])]
            if "listed" in filters:
                listed = _bool("listed", filters["listed"])
                stocks = [s for s in stocks if s["listed"] is listed]
            doc = self._envelope(snap, *stocks_meta, stocks)
        elif route == "/v2/stocks/{instrument_code}":
            code = params["instrument_code"]
            entry = next((s for s in snap.stocks if s["instrument_code"] == code), None)
            if entry is None:
                raise HttpError(404, f"Unknown instrument_code: {code}")
            own = [a for a in snap.actions if a["instrument_code"] == code]
            data = {**entry, "corporate_actions": own, "dividends": [a for a in own if a["type"] == "dividend"]}
            doc = self._envelope(snap, *snap.stock_meta[code], data)
        elif route == "/v2/indices":
            doc = self._envelope(snap, *dataset("indices"), list(snap.indices))
        else:  # /v2/corporate-actions and /v2/dividends
            actions = list(snap.actions)
            if route == "/v2/dividends":
                actions = [a for a in actions if a["type"] == "dividend"]
                if "upcoming" in filters:
                    upcoming = _bool("upcoming", filters["upcoming"])
                    today = self.server.today().isoformat()
                    actions = [a for a in actions
                               if (a["payment_date"] is not None and a["payment_date"] >= today) is upcoming]
            if "symbol" in filters:
                actions = [a for a in actions if _same(a["symbol"], filters["symbol"])]
            if "type" in filters:
                actions = [a for a in actions if _same(a["type"], filters["type"])]
            doc = self._envelope(snap, *dataset("corporate_actions"), actions)
        self._json(200, doc)
```

---

### Task 16: CLI and background refresh

**Why:** ties everything together (spec §3.3, §6.1, §6.3):
- **Commands:** `jamstockex` / `jamstockex serve` loads the data, starts the server and, unless `--offline`, runs a background thread. The thread checks at startup and then every `check_every_minutes`: it reloads the file (to pick up a `jamstockex refresh` run in the meantime), compares clocks, and refreshes under `RefreshLock` only when something is due and allowed. Then it saves and swaps the snapshot. `jamstockex refresh [--listings]` runs one refresh, prints the report and exits 0 or 1. `--version` prints `jamstockex 2.0.0`.
- **Data folder:** macOS `~/Library/Application Support/jamstockex/`; Windows `%APPDATA%\jamstockex\`; Linux `$XDG_DATA_HOME/jamstockex/` (only if absolute), else `~/.local/share/jamstockex/`. `--data-dir` overrides it.
- **Exit codes:** a port in use exits 2 with `Port <n> is in use; try --port <n+1>`. A config or data-file error exits 2, naming the file. A held lock makes `refresh` exit 1 with `another refresh is running`, while `serve` skips that check.
- **Startup output:** the three lines from spec §3.3. The blocked message is printed when a refresh is blocked.

**Files:**
- Create: `jamstockex/__main__.py`
- Test: `tests/test_cli.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `load_config`, `ConfigError`, `Config` (Task 2); `Fetcher` (Task 4); `store.load`, `store.save`, `store.RefreshLock`, `store.LockHeld`, `store.StoreError`, `store.DATA_FILE`, `store.LOCK_FILE` (Task 10); `due`, `run_refresh`, `Report`, `BLOCKED_MESSAGE` (Tasks 12–13); `make_server`, `Snapshot`, `LocalServer` (Task 15); `tests.fakes.FakeFetcher`, `pages` (Task 13).
- Produces:
  - `utcnow() -> datetime` (patched in tests); `Fetcher` is imported into the module namespace so tests can patch `jamstockex.__main__.Fetcher`
  - `default_data_dir(platform: str | None = None, env: dict[str, str] | None = None, home: Path | None = None) -> Path`
  - `build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser`; `main(argv: list[str] | None = None, *, on_ready: Callable[[LocalServer], None] | None = None) -> int`
  - `cmd_refresh(config: Config, data_dir: Path, *, force_listings: bool) -> int`; `cmd_serve(config: Config, data_dir: Path, *, port: int, offline: bool, on_ready=None) -> int`
  - `class Service(config: Config, data_dir: Path, state: State)` with `snapshot() -> Snapshot`, `check() -> Report | None` (`None` when nothing ran) and `run(stop: threading.Event) -> None`
  - `python -m jamstockex` runs `sys.exit(main())`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`tests/test_cli.py`:

```python
import datetime as dt
import http.client
import json
import queue
import socket
import threading
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

import jamstockex.__main__ as cli
from jamstockex import store
from jamstockex.config import JAMAICA, default_config
from jamstockex.fetch import BlockedError
from jamstockex.refresh import BLOCKED_MESSAGE
from tests.fakes import FakeFetcher, pages

NOW = dt.datetime(2026, 11, 9, 18, 0, tzinfo=JAMAICA)  # Monday, after the close


@pytest.fixture
def fake_site(monkeypatch):
    """Patches the CLI's Fetcher and clock. Returns the list of fetchers the CLI created."""
    created = []

    def factory(source, page_map=None):
        fetcher = FakeFetcher(pages(page_map))
        created.append(fetcher)
        return fetcher

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "Fetcher", factory)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "utcnow", lambda: NOW)
    return created


@pytest.fixture
def no_network(monkeypatch):
    def refuse(source):
        raise AssertionError("no Fetcher may be created")

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "Fetcher", refuse)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "utcnow", lambda: NOW)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("platform, env, expected", [
    ("darwin", {}, "/home/u/Library/Application Support/jamstockex"),
    ("win32", {"APPDATA": "/appdata"}, "/appdata/jamstockex"),
    ("win32", {}, "/home/u/AppData/Roaming/jamstockex"),
    ("linux", {"XDG_DATA_HOME": "/xdg"}, "/xdg/jamstockex"),
    ("linux", {"XDG_DATA_HOME": "relative/path"}, "/home/u/.local/share/jamstockex"),
    ("linux", {}, "/home/u/.local/share/jamstockex"),
])
def test_default_data_dir(platform, env, expected):
    assert cli.default_data_dir(platform=platform, env=env, home=Path("/home/u")) == Path(expected)


def test_version(capsys):
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as caught:
        cli.main(["--version"])
    assert caught.value.code == 0
    assert capsys.readouterr().out.strip() == "jamstockex 2.0.0"


def test_there_is_no_host_option(capsys):
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as caught:
        cli.main(["--host", "0.0.0.0"])
    assert caught.value.code == 2


def test_config_error_exits_2_naming_the_file(tmp_path, capsys, no_network):
    (tmp_path / "config.toml").write_text("[server]\nport = 0\n", encoding="utf-8")
    assert cli.main(["refresh", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path)]) == 2
    err = capsys.readouterr().err
    assert str(tmp_path / "config.toml") in err and "server.port" in err


def test_corrupt_data_file_exits_2(tmp_path, capsys, no_network):
    (tmp_path / "jse-data.json").write_text("{oops", encoding="utf-8")
    assert cli.main(["--offline", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path), "--port", "0"]) == 2
    assert "left untouched" in capsys.readouterr().err
    assert (tmp_path / "jse-data.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "{oops"


def test_refresh_saves_and_prints_the_report(tmp_path, capsys, fake_site):
    assert cli.main(["refresh", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path)]) == 0
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert "JSE refresh for trading day 2026-11-09" in out and "Requests to the JSE site: 6" in out
    state = store.load(tmp_path / "jse-data.json")
    assert set(state.companies) == {"9001", "9002"}
    assert not (tmp_path / "jse-data.lock").exists()

    assert cli.main(["refresh", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path)]) == 0  # same close: nothing due
    assert "Nothing is due" in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert fake_site[-1].paths == []


def test_refresh_listings_flag_forces_all_markets(tmp_path, capsys, fake_site):
    cli.main(["refresh", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path)])
    cli.main(["refresh", "--listings", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path)])
    assert [p for p in fake_site[-1].paths if p.startswith("/listings/")] == [
        f"/listings/listed-companies/?market={code}" for code in (31, 22, 51, 49)]


def test_refresh_blocked_exits_1_with_the_blocked_message(tmp_path, capsys, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "Fetcher", lambda source: FakeFetcher(pages({"/trading/trade-quotes/": BlockedError("HTTP 403")})))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "utcnow", lambda: NOW)
    assert cli.main(["refresh", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path)]) == 1
    assert BLOCKED_MESSAGE in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert store.load(tmp_path / "jse-data.json").blocked_close is not None


def test_refresh_exits_1_when_another_refresh_holds_the_lock(tmp_path, capsys, no_network):
    with store.RefreshLock(tmp_path / "jse-data.lock"):
        assert cli.main(["refresh", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path)]) == 1
    assert "another refresh is running" in capsys.readouterr().err


def test_port_in_use_exits_2(tmp_path, capsys, no_network):
    with socket.socket() as busy:
        busy.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        busy.listen()
        port = busy.getsockname()[1]
        assert cli.main(["--offline", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path), "--port", str(port)]) == 2
    assert f"Port {port} is in use; try --port {port + 1}" in capsys.readouterr().err


def start_serving(argv):
    """Runs cli.main(argv) in a thread; returns (server, thread, result queue)."""
    ready, result = queue.Queue(), queue.Queue()
    thread = threading.Thread(target=lambda: result.put(cli.main(argv, on_ready=ready.put)), daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    return ready.get(timeout=10), thread, result


def get(server, path):
    conn = http.client.HTTPConnection("127.0.0.1", server.port, timeout=5)
    conn.request("GET", path)
    response = conn.getresponse()
    body = response.read()
    conn.close()
    return response.status, body


def test_serve_offline_makes_no_fetcher_calls(tmp_path, capsys, no_network):
    server, thread, result = start_serving(["--offline", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path), "--port", "0"])
    status, body = get(server, "/v2/status")
    assert status == 200
    assert json.loads(body)["data"]["data_file"] == str(tmp_path / "jse-data.json")
    server.shutdown()
    thread.join(timeout=10)
    assert result.get(timeout=1) == 0
    out = capsys.readouterr().out.splitlines()
    assert out[0] == f"JSE data API running at http://127.0.0.1:{server.port}/v2/   docs: http://127.0.0.1:{server.port}/docs"
    assert out[1] == f"Data: {tmp_path / 'jse-data.json'} (as of no data yet)"
    assert out[2] == "Offline: not refreshing (Ctrl+C to stop)"


def test_serve_refreshes_in_the_background(tmp_path, capsys, fake_site):
    server, thread, result = start_serving(["serve", "--data-dir", str(tmp_path), "--port", "0"])
    deadline = dt.datetime.now() + dt.timedelta(seconds=10)
    while True:
        status, body = get(server, "/v2/stocks")
        if json.loads(body)["data"] or dt.datetime.now() > deadline:
            break
        threading.Event().wait(0.05)
    assert [s["instrument_code"] for s in json.loads(body)["data"]] == ["9001", "9002"]
    server.shutdown()
    thread.join(timeout=10)
    assert result.get(timeout=1) == 0
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert "Refreshing in background… (Ctrl+C to stop)" in out
    assert "Refreshed: data as of 2026-11-09 (6 request(s) to the JSE site)." in out
    assert store.load(tmp_path / "jse-data.json").status["quotes"].as_of == dt.date(2026, 11, 9)


def test_service_check_is_a_clock_comparison_when_nothing_is_due(tmp_path, fake_site):
    service = cli.Service(default_config(), tmp_path, store.State())
    assert service.check() is not None  # first check refreshes
    assert service.check() is None      # same close: no fetcher created
    assert len(fake_site) == 1


def test_service_does_not_retry_after_a_block_until_the_next_close(tmp_path, capsys, monkeypatch):
    created = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "Fetcher", lambda source: created.append(1) or FakeFetcher(
        pages({"/trading/trade-quotes/": BlockedError("HTTP 403")})))
    clock = [NOW]
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "utcnow", lambda: clock[0])
    service = cli.Service(default_config(), tmp_path, store.State())
    assert service.check().blocked
    assert BLOCKED_MESSAGE in capsys.readouterr().out
    clock[0] = NOW + dt.timedelta(hours=20)  # Tuesday 14:00, before the next close
    assert service.check() is None
    assert len(created) == 1
    clock[0] = dt.datetime(2026, 11, 10, 17, 30, tzinfo=JAMAICA)
    assert service.check() is not None
    assert len(created) == 2


def test_service_skips_a_check_while_another_refresh_holds_the_lock(tmp_path, capsys, no_network):
    service = cli.Service(default_config(), tmp_path, store.State())
    with store.RefreshLock(tmp_path / "jse-data.lock"):
        assert service.check() is None
    assert "Another refresh is running" in capsys.readouterr().err
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the CLI**

`jamstockex/__main__.py`:

```python
"""CLI: `jamstockex [serve] [--port N] [--offline]`, `jamstockex refresh [--listings]`, `--version`.

Every command takes `--data-dir`; the default is the per-user data folder.
Exit codes: 0 ok; 1 a dataset failed, the site blocked us, or another refresh
holds the lock; 2 a config, data-file or port problem.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import errno
import logging
import os
import sys
import threading
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

from jamstockex import __version__, store
from jamstockex.config import Config, ConfigError, load_config
from jamstockex.fetch import Fetcher
from jamstockex.refresh import BLOCKED_MESSAGE, Report, due, run_refresh
from jamstockex.server import LocalServer, Snapshot, make_server

log = logging.getLogger("jamstockex")


def utcnow() -> dt.datetime:
    return dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc)


def default_data_dir(platform: str | None = None, env: dict[str, str] | None = None,
                     home: Path | None = None) -> Path:
    platform = sys.platform if platform is None else platform
    env = dict(os.environ) if env is None else env
    home = Path.home() if home is None else home
    if platform == "darwin":
        return home / "Library" / "Application Support" / "jamstockex"
    if platform == "win32":
        appdata = env.get("APPDATA")
        return (Path(appdata) if appdata else home / "AppData" / "Roaming") / "jamstockex"
    xdg = env.get("XDG_DATA_HOME", "")
    if xdg and Path(xdg).is_absolute():
        return Path(xdg) / "jamstockex"
    return home / ".local" / "share" / "jamstockex"


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="jamstockex",
        description="Keeps JSE end-of-day data in a local file and serves it on http://127.0.0.1 only.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--version", action="version", version=f"jamstockex {__version__}")
    parser.add_argument("--data-dir", help="data folder (default: the per-user data folder)")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, help="serve: port on 127.0.0.1 (default 8765)")
    parser.add_argument("--offline", action="store_true", help="serve: don't refresh")
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", metavar="{serve,refresh}")
    serve = commands.add_parser("serve", help="serve the API and refresh in the background (the default)")
    serve.add_argument("--data-dir", default=argparse.SUPPRESS)
    serve.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=argparse.SUPPRESS)
    serve.add_argument("--offline", action="store_true", default=argparse.SUPPRESS)
    refresh = commands.add_parser("refresh", help="refresh what is due now, print the report, and exit")
    refresh.add_argument("--data-dir", default=argparse.SUPPRESS)
    refresh.add_argument("--listings", action="store_true", help="also refresh every market's listings")
    return parser


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None, *, on_ready: Callable[[LocalServer], None] | None = None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
            stream.reconfigure(errors="replace")
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(message)s")
    data_dir = Path(args.data_dir) if args.data_dir else default_data_dir()
    try:
        config = load_config(data_dir)
    except ConfigError as exc:
        print(f"Config error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    if args.command == "refresh":
        return cmd_refresh(config, data_dir, force_listings=args.listings)
    return cmd_serve(config, data_dir, port=config.port if args.port is None else args.port,
                     offline=args.offline, on_ready=on_ready)


def cmd_refresh(config: Config, data_dir: Path, *, force_listings: bool) -> int:
    data_file = data_dir / store.DATA_FILE
    try:
        with store.RefreshLock(data_dir / store.LOCK_FILE):
            state = store.load(data_file)
            report = run_refresh(state, Fetcher(config.source), utcnow(), config, force_listings=force_listings)
            if report.attempted:
                store.save(state, data_file, now=utcnow())
    except store.LockHeld:
        print("another refresh is running", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    except store.StoreError as exc:
        print(f"Data file error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    print(report.text())
    return report.exit_code


class Service:
    """Holds the served snapshot and runs automatic refreshes for `serve`."""

    def __init__(self, config: Config, data_dir: Path, state: store.State) -> None:
        self.config = config
        self.data_file = data_dir / store.DATA_FILE
        self.lock_file = data_dir / store.LOCK_FILE
        self.state = state
        self.refreshing = False
        self.next_check_at: dt.datetime | None = None
        self._snapshot = Snapshot.from_state(state, data_file=str(self.data_file))

    def snapshot(self) -> Snapshot:
        return self._snapshot

    def _publish(self) -> None:
        self._snapshot = Snapshot.from_state(self.state, data_file=str(self.data_file)).with_runtime(
            refreshing=self.refreshing, next_check_at=self.next_check_at)

    def check(self) -> Report | None:
        """One check: a clock comparison, then a refresh only if one is due and allowed."""
        now = utcnow()
        try:
            self.state = store.load(self.data_file)  # pick up a `jamstockex refresh` run meanwhile
        except store.StoreError as exc:
            print(f"Data file error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
            return None
        self._publish()
        if not due(self.state, now, self.config).automatic_allowed(now):
            return None
        lock = store.RefreshLock(self.lock_file)
        try:
            lock.acquire()
        except store.LockHeld:
            print("Another refresh is running; skipping this check.", file=sys.stderr)
            return None
        try:
            self.refreshing = True
            self._publish()
            report = run_refresh(self.state, Fetcher(self.config.source), now, self.config)
            if report.attempted:
                store.save(self.state, self.data_file, now=utcnow())
        finally:
            self.refreshing = False
            lock.release()
            self._publish()
        if report.blocked:
            print(BLOCKED_MESSAGE, flush=True)
        elif report.errors:
            print(f"Refresh failed for {', '.join(sorted(report.errors))}; will retry in "
                  f"{self.config.refresh.retry_after_failure_minutes} minutes.", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"Refreshed: data as of {report.trade_date.isoformat()} "
                  f"({report.requests} request(s) to the JSE site).", flush=True)
        return report

    def run(self, stop: threading.Event) -> None:
        interval = self.config.refresh.check_every_minutes * 60
        while not stop.is_set():
            try:
                self.check()
            except Exception:
                log.exception("Background refresh failed")
            self.next_check_at = utcnow() + dt.timedelta(seconds=interval)
            self._publish()
            stop.wait(interval)


def cmd_serve(config: Config, data_dir: Path, *, port: int, offline: bool,
              on_ready: Callable[[LocalServer], None] | None = None) -> int:
    data_file = data_dir / store.DATA_FILE
    try:
        state = store.load(data_file)
    except store.StoreError as exc:
        print(f"Data file error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    service = Service(config, data_dir, state)
    try:
        server = make_server(port, service.snapshot)
    except OSError as exc:
        if exc.errno == errno.EADDRINUSE:
            print(f"Port {port} is in use; try --port {port + 1}", file=sys.stderr)
        else:
            print(f"Can't listen on 127.0.0.1:{port}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.port}"
    as_of = state.status["quotes"].as_of or state.status["listings"].as_of
    print(f"JSE data API running at {base}/v2/   docs: {base}/docs")
    print(f"Data: {data_file} (as of {as_of.isoformat() if as_of else 'no data yet'})")
    print("Offline: not refreshing (Ctrl+C to stop)" if offline else "Refreshing in background… (Ctrl+C to stop)",
          flush=True)
    stop = threading.Event()
    worker = None
    if not offline:
        worker = threading.Thread(target=service.run, args=(stop,), name="refresh", daemon=True)
        worker.start()
    if on_ready is not None:
        on_ready(server)
    try:
        server.serve_forever(poll_interval=0.2)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("Stopping.")
    finally:
        stop.set()
        if worker is not None:
            worker.join(timeout=5)
        server.server_close()
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```
